# NocoBase 3 application development

Use this Skill when building a feature in this application: a page, an endpoint, a table, a service, or the tests covering them.

Do not use it to develop a published plugin package. Plugin development has its own protocol and lives in a separate repository.

Do not use it to upgrade the template this application was generated from. That is `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-upgrade/`, which reconciles a newer template release against the application without reverting the user's work.

## Before you start

Read the application's `AGENTS.md` first for the rules that apply everywhere. This Skill's references are the detail behind it. Before running any `pnpm nocobase` command, read [the command line](references/cli.md): it maps tasks to commands and states the flags an agent must not add on its own.

Confirm you are in an application and not a plugin package. An application has:

```text
client/runtime.ts
server/runtime.ts
config.example.yml
```

If instead you find `client/plugin.ts`, `server/plugin.ts`, or a `defineServerPlugin()` declaration, you are in a plugin package and this Skill does not apply.

Do not infer the application's capabilities from a template name or from this Skill. Inspect `package.json`, `client/plugins.ts`, `server/plugins.ts`, `cli/plugins.ts`, `server/config/index.ts`, and `database/` before deciding what is installed, registered, configured, or application-owned. `nocobase.templatePackage` and `nocobase.templateKind` identify known template ancestry when present, but a new template remains valid without being listed here. Read [template variants](references/template-variants.md) only when those files identify one of the documented official templates.

## The one rule that decides most tasks

Build the feature in the application. Do not run a plugin generator, create a `packages/plugins/` directory, or write a `defineServerPlugin()` declaration for this application's own feature. Create a plugin only when the user explicitly asks for an independently published, reusable package.

## Check the installed plugins first

NocoBase packages may publish Skills under `.agents/skills/`. Current application templates run `pnpm nocobase skills sync` automatically through `postinstall`; run it manually if install scripts were disabled or that directory is missing or stale. Confirm that the package is a direct `@nocobase/*` dependency or a registered plugin before relying on its Skill. The common capability mappings are:

| The requirement sounds like                                                                   | Read the Skill for                    |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| Approvals, multi-step processes, "when X happens then Y"                                      | `@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow`       |
| An assistant in the app: chat, reading files dropped into it, acting through tools you define | `@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee`    |
| Email, IM, or in-app messages                                                                 | `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification`   |
| Roles, permissions, per-user or per-record access                                             | `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization`  |
| Sign-in, registration, sessions                                                               | `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication` |
| File upload and metadata through Repository                                                   | `@nocobase/app-plugin-file`           |
| Translated text and language switching                                                        | `@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n`           |
| User administration and application-owned role assignment                                     | `@nocobase/app-plugin-users`          |
| Reading or writing data, schema changes, migrations                                           | `@nocobase/db`                        |

Read the relevant Skill before writing the feature, but treat this table as a map rather than an installed-package list. Implementing a permission system, a notification sender, or a scheduler by hand when a registered plugin provides one is the most expensive mistake available here.

For notification configuration or sending, follow the notification plugin Skill: `notification.channels` maps each name to one flat Provider configuration, and `send({ idempotencyKey, messages })` supplies a complete message per Channel. Use native addresses; email and in-app arrays create independent deliveries, while Webhooks forbid `to`.

Skills synchronization reads direct `@nocobase/*` dependencies from the application manifest and retains compatibility with explicitly registered plugins. It does not make an unregistered runtime plugin active; the composition roots remain the authority for registration and contribution order.

  buildFilter<LibraryDocument>((f) =>
    f.and([

    ]),

      .title({ key: 'library.access.owned', ns: APP_NAMESPACE })
      .collections(LIBRARY_COLLECTION)
      .resolver(({ principal }) =>
        principal.type === 'user'
          ? buildFilter<LibraryDocument>((f) =>
              f.string('ownerId').eq(principal.id),
            )
          : false,
      ),
);

/** Published, non-confidential documents: the open shelf. */
export const publishedDocumentsAccess = defineRecordAccess(
  'library.published',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title({ key: 'library.access.published', ns: APP_NAMESPACE })

    E90 · app/server/library/record-access.ts:72–95

    E92 · app/server/routes/library.ts:136–187

    路由在读取输入前先 policyFor 取操作策略，deny 即抛 PERMISSION_DENIED；repository = database.repository(...).withPolicy(libraryPolicy) 把策略落到 Repository。

          authorization.database.policyFor(
        LIBRARY_COLLECTION,
        context.get('authz'),
        { resource: 'library.documents', action },
      );

    /**
     * Resolves and stores the operation's policy, denying before any input is read.
     *
     * The stored policy is the caller's *view* policy with the operation's own
     * permission swapped in, because a write returns the row it wrote and the
     * Repository has to be allowed to read it back. Reading it back through the
     * view policy is what a later `GET` would do, so the write cannot return a
     * row the caller could not read.
     */
    const documentsAccess =
      (action: LibraryAction): MiddlewareHandler<LibraryEnv> =>
      async (context, next) => {
        const operationPolicy = await policyFor(context, action);
        const permission = CRUD_BY_ACTION[action];
        if (operationPolicy[permission] === false) {
          throw new ApiError({
            status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
            reason: `LIBRARY_${action.toUpperCase()}_DENIED`,
            domain: 'library',
            message: `The ${action} operation on the document library is not allowed.`,
          });
        }
        const viewPolicy =
          action === 'view'
            ? operationPolicy
            : await policyFor(context, 'view');
        context.set('libraryPolicy', {
          ...viewPolicy,
          [permission]: operationPolicy[permission],
        });
        await next();
      };

    const repository = (context: {
      get: (key: 'libraryPolicy') => RepositoryPolicy;
    }) =>
      database
        .repository(LIBRARY_COLLECTION)
        .withPolicy(context.get('libraryPolicy'));

    const currentUserId = (context: {
      get: (key: 'authz') => AuthorizationEnv['Variables']['authz'];
    }) => context.get('authz').identity.principal.id;

    routes.get(
      '/',

    原文件 SHA-256：b68ed3588581a383a32b00c347f3fdceaaa892cd5a76a077c2e0f4ac56c064b0

    E96 · app/database/main/migrations/202610100001_create_library_documents.ts:10–29

    library_documents 表：title/body/published/confidential/ownerId，以及 ownerId、可见性索引。

        await builder.createCollection('libraryDocuments', (collection) => {
      // A string primary key, like the `user` table, so record selections and
      // route parameters address a document the same way everywhere.
      collection.string('id', { length: 64 }).notNull();
      collection.string('title', { length: 255 }).notNull();
      collection.text('body').nullable();
      collection.boolean('published').notNull().defaultTo(false);
      collection.boolean('confidential').notNull().defaultTo(false);
      collection
        .string('ownerId', { length: 64 })
        .notNull()
        .references({ collection: 'user', field: 'id' });
      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();
      collection.primary('id', { name: 'pk_library_documents' });
      collection.index('ownerId', { name: 'idx_library_documents_owner' });
      collection.index(['published', 'confidential'], {
        name: 'idx_library_documents_visibility',
      });
    });

    What was delivered

    业务场景与交付说明

    角色边界

    阅读者乙仅能阅读已发布不保密的公开资料P，无新建与编辑入口，更新请求被服务端拒绝；资料员甲可维护自己负责的资料，编辑时原值正确回填并成功保存。

    单条临时共享

    管理员通过 Sharing Rules 以 Specific record 方式只把私有草稿D开放给乙；乙可读D但无编辑权，其他记录仍不可见，撤销后刷新或原链接均看不到正文。

    保密与共享隔离

    即使把保密资料C共享给乙，乙在列表和详情都看不到内容，而甲对C的访问不受影响。

    账号停用

    管理员用既有 User management 停用乙后，乙原有会话被重定向到登录页，甲账号与资料不受影响；全程无页面或控制台错误。

    01管理员重置测试账号密码并登录
    →
    02乙只读公开资料P，甲维护自有资料
    →
    03管理员按单条记录共享草稿D给乙
    →
    04乙只读查看D，其他记录仍不可见
    →
    05管理员撤销D共享，乙刷新后不可见
    →
    06给乙共享保密资料C，乙仍看不到
    →
    07管理员停用乙，其旧会话失效，甲不受影响

    业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

    01

    A1 · 6 条操作 · 5 条观察 · 4 张证据
    Agent 报告通过
    ⌄

    原始验收要求

    乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

    实际操作

    1. 以管理员登录后重置阅读者乙与资料员甲的密码，分别用独立浏览器会话登录乙(reader@example.com)和甲(librarian@example.com)。
    2. 乙打开 /main/library：列表只显示已发布不保密的“公开资料 P”，无“New document”按钮。
    3. 乙打开 P 详情：正文可见，详情页无 Edit/Delete 按钮；乙用自身会话 PATCH /api/library/documents/<P-id> 被服务端拒绝(404 Not Found)。
    4. 甲打开 /main/library：可见全部三条资料且有“New document”与逐行 Actions。
    5. 甲对 P 点击 Actions→Edit：表单回填原标题“公开资料 P”、原正文、Published 开关开启、Confidential 关闭；改动正文并 Save。
    6. 重新打开 P 详情：正文显示已更新的“甲已维护。”，Updated 时间更新，且显示 Edit/Delete，证明保存成功。

    观察结果

    1. 乙的资料库仅列出“公开资料 P”，无新建按钮(b01-reader-library.png)。
    2. 乙打开 P 可读到正文，详情底部无 Edit/Delete(b01-reader-p-read.png)。
    3. 乙的 PATCH 更新返回 404 Not found，无法修改。
    4. 甲编辑 P 时原标题、原正文、Published/Confidential 开关均正确回填(b01-librarian-edit-prefill.png)。
    5. 保存后 P 详情显示修改后的正文且时间更新，并有 Edit/Delete(b01-librarian-p-updated.png)。

    来源：verify-5/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

    02

    乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

    乙打开 C 仍被拒绝，未获得其他记录访问权。

    A2 · 5 条操作 · 5 条观察 · 5 张证据
    Agent 报告通过
    ⌄

    原始验收要求

    乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

    实际操作

    1. 开放前：乙用自身会话直接打开 D 的原始 URL /main/library/<D-id>。
    2. 管理员在 Settings→Authorization→Sharing Rules 新建规则：名称“Temporary share D to reader”、Key share-d-reader、资源 Document library、Applies to 阅读者乙、仅勾选 View documents、记录范围 Specific record IDs 选中“私有草稿 D”，保存。
    3. 乙完整重新加载 /main/library：列表新增显示“私有草稿 D”，仍只有 D 与 P，未出现保密资料 C。
    4. 乙打开 D 详情：正文可见；详情页无 Edit/Delete 按钮；乙 PATCH D 记录被服务端拒绝 404。
    5. 乙直接打开 C 的原始 URL：仍显示 “This document no longer exists.”，说明共享只扩展了 D 这一条记录。

    观察结果

    1. 开放前乙打开 D 显示 “This document no longer exists.”(b02-reader-d-denied-before.png)。
    2. 管理员共享表单选中阅读者乙、View documents、Specific record D(b02-admin-share-d-form.png)；保存后规则列表显示 1 selected records(b02-admin-rule-created.png)。
    3. 开放后乙列表出现“私有草稿 D”(b02-reader-d-listed.png)。
    4. 乙能读到 D 正文且无 Edit/Delete 按钮(b02-reader-d-read-noedit.png)，PATCH D 返回 404。
    5. 乙打开 C 仍被拒绝，未获得其他记录访问权。

    原始验收要求

    停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。

    实际操作

    1. 确认乙已在独立会话登录并处于可用状态后，管理员在 Settings→User management 对阅读者乙打开 User actions→Disable account，确认提示“阅读者乙 will be signed out from every device immediately.”并点击 Disable。
    2. 用户列表显示阅读者乙状态为 Disabled，资料员甲与 Super Admin 仍为 Enabled。
    3. 乙在原会话（未重新登录）打开 /main/library：被重定向到 /main/login 登录页，原有会话无法继续访问。
    4. 甲在自身会话打开 /main/library：仍显示全部三条资料、New document 与行内 Actions，账号和资料不受影响。

    观察结果

    1. 用户列表中阅读者乙显示 Disabled，其他账号 Enabled(b05-admin-reader-disabled.png)。
    2. 乙原会话访问资料库被重定向到登录页(b05-reader-session-invalidated.png)。
    3. 甲仍能正常访问资料库与全部资料(b05-librarian-unaffected.png)。

    来源：verify-5/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

    完整原始验收要求
    B01. 乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

B02. 乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

B03. 撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

B04. 即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

B05. 停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。
